  While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for this task, existing methods often rely on static inspiration corpora, predefined heuristics, or laborious human-in-the-loop pipelines and decision-support frameworks that limit scalability and novelty. In this work, we propose an automated approach, a Memory-augmented, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded (MAIL) framework for hypothesis generation in chemistry. Our MAIL method formulates hypothesis generation as a temporally grounded, memory-driven reasoning process, where hypotheses emerge from an evolving conceptual path that continuously accumulates and reinterprets prior knowledge. We evaluated the MAIL framework on a public TOMATO-Chem dataset and a newly curated and disseminated high-novelty nature/science challenge (HN-NS) dataset. Across both datasets, MAIL generates structurally coherent and mechanistically plausible hypotheses, achieves the highest MIOS and MPOS by more effectively recovering the central ideas and methodological elements of the historical target hypotheses, and obtains the highest overall expert-evaluation scores for scientific quality. These results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to autonomously explore chemical domains and generate hypotheses that are both innovative and chemically plausible.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Covering 1024 syndromes with 50 columns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27494)
*Stephen Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该摘要报告构造了一个新的二元线性 [50,40]_2 覆盖码，覆盖半径为 2，从而改进了已知上界 ℓ_2(10,2)≤50，并通过既有构造推广到更长码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖半径为 2 的二元线性码长度上界长期由 Kaikkonen--Rosendahl 的长度 51 构造保持；进一步缩短长度可改进参数表，并影响后续基于该种子码的构造族。

Method: 作者给出一个 [50,40]_2 校验矩阵/码构造，验证其覆盖半径为 2；该矩阵还具有可分成十个块的 (2,0)-partition，因此可使用 Construction QM_2^2 递推出更长长度的码，并提供矩阵、验证器和源码以支持复现。

Result: 得到 ℓ_2(10,2)≤50，较 2003 年长度 51 的结果少一列；进一步得到 r=18 和 r=20 时长度 815 与 1631 的经穷尽验证码，以及一般族 n=51·2^{r/2-5}-1，其渐近密度为 2601/2048。

Conclusion: 该工作在二元覆盖码覆盖半径 2 的长度上界上取得小但关键的改进，并通过分块结构将改进传播到无限码族，具有参数表更新和可复现验证价值。

Abstract: We exhibit a binary linear $[50,40]_2$ code of covering radius $2$, so $\ell_2(10,2)\le 50$, one column below the Kaikkonen--Rosendahl length $51$ that has stood since 2003 and that still seeds the $R=2$ family of Davydov--Marcugini--Pambianco (arXiv:2511.02542). The new matrix admits a $(2,0)$-partition into ten blocks, so Construction $\mathrm{QM}_2^2$ propagates it to exhaustively verified codes of lengths $815$ and $1631$ at $r=18$ and $r=20$, and to the family $n=51\cdot 2^{r/2-5}-1$ of asymptotic density $2601/2048$. The matrices, verifiers, and source are at https://github.com/wustep/maths, pin problems/covering/share/2026-08-24/ at commit 736a38f.

</details>


### [212] [Selective Interference Suppression of Siamese-Net in Heterogeneous Interference Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27635)
*Arkadeep Sinha,Shubham Paul,R. Manivasakan,Nambi Seshadri,R. David Koilpillai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究多用户实高斯干扰信道中的端到端学习短块码，发现Siamese式耦合训练可对强干扰用户对进行选择性正交化，而非强制全局正交，从而在编码增益与误块率鲁棒性之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构干扰强度的多用户通信场景中，只有少数用户对存在主导干扰。作者希望弄清楚端到端学习的SiameseNet是否能根据干扰结构自适应地抑制主要干扰，还是会不必要地对所有用户码本施加全局正交性，进而损失编码增益。

Method: 作者考察一个4用户不等干扰配置，其中用户对(1,2)具有强干扰Λ，其余或部分用户对具有弱背景耦合λ；在各接收端保持单用户解码。通过端到端学习短块码，并使用潜在空间跨用户相似性统计量，例如最坏情形相干性和平均相似度，分析学习码本的几何结构及其与BLER表现的关系。

Result: 实验或分析表明，学习到的码本主要在强干扰用户对之间趋于近正交，而弱耦合用户对仍保留一定对齐结构以维持编码增益。该选择性几何结构与不等干扰条件下更稳健的BLER性能相对应。

Conclusion: SiameseNet并非简单地强制所有用户全局正交，而是能够根据不同用户对的干扰强度进行选择性干扰抑制，从而实现由正交性与编码增益共同决定的性能权衡。

Abstract: We study an end-to-end learnt short-block codes for a $N$-user real Gaussian interference channel with heterogeneous pairwise interference strengths, while keeping single-user decoding at every receiver. In this paper, we study the case wherein only a few dominant interferers exist and investigate whether Siamese-style coupled training can adapt selectively to encode (\& decode) to ensure optimal performance corresponding to best tradeoff between orthogonality and coding gain or it enforces unnecessary global orthogonality oblivious of the reality. Our work focuses on a 4-user unequal-interference configuration with one dominant pair $(1,2)$ (of strength $Λ$) and a weak pair (with weak background coupling ($λ$)), through which we demonstrate a selective interference suppression phenomenon where the learned codebooks become near-orthogonal primarily for the dominant pair, while weakly coupled pairs retain alignment needed for coding gain. We quantify this behaviour using latent-space cross-user similarity statistics (worst-case coherence measure, average similarity measure, etc) and connect these geometric signatures to the observed BLER robustness under unequal interference. It seems that the SiameseNet selectively suppresses the interferences from various interferring user pairs to yield optimal tradeoff between coding gain and BLER dictated by orthogonality.

</details>


### [213] [Low-Altitude Fluid Antenna Network with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27909)
*Tong Zhang,Yanfei Su,Shuai Wang,Wanli Ni,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向低空无线网络的流体天线快速重构方案，利用电磁数字孪生辅助的多智能体强化学习，并通过两阶段迁移学习缩小仿真到真实部署的差距；案例显示相较固定天线位置基线，系统和速率提升118.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络需要同时服务无人机与eVTOL等低空平台，但空地/空空信道高速变化、突发遮挡和异构干扰会显著影响通信、感知与定位性能。流体天线可通过重构天线位置提供额外空间自由度，但其在动态低空场景中的快速高性能控制仍具挑战。

Method: 作者构建电磁数字孪生辅助的多智能体强化学习框架，用于联合优化流体天线位置与波束成形；同时设计两阶段迁移学习机制，以缓解训练环境与真实部署之间的sim-to-real差距，实现毫秒级动态重构。

Result: 案例研究表明，联合优化流体天线位置和波束成形可使系统总速率相比固定位置基线提升118.5%，收益来自流体天线阵列的毫秒级重构和对移动空中用户的自适应波束指向。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，电磁数字孪生、MARL与迁移学习结合，有望推动低空流体天线网络的实际落地，并显著增强动态低空通信场景下的吞吐性能和自适应能力。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) integrate terrestrial and aerial platforms to provide ubiquitous communication, sensing, and localization services for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. However, dynamic air-ground and air-air channels, abrupt blockages, and heterogeneous interference hinder the realization of this goal. Nevertheless, fluid antenna (FA), a cutting-edge multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technique, overcomes these challenges by reconfiguring antenna positions to unlock additional spatial degrees-of-freedom. In this paper, towards bringing low-altitude FA networks into reality, we study the fast and high-performance FA reconfiguration for low-altitude FA networks with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Specifically, we present an electromagnetic digital twin (EM-DT)-assisted MARL framework. To fill the sim-to-real gap, we introduce a two-stage transfer learning framework. Our case study shows that joint FA positions and beamforming optimization can enhance the system sum-rate by 118.5%, compared to the fixed position baseline. This gain comes from the dynamic millisecond timescale reconfiguration of FA arrays and the adaptive steering of beams toward aerial users with mobility.

</details>


### [214] [Finite Sample Bounds for Composite Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28068)
*El{í}as Vera-Sig{ü}enza,Amedeo Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限样本下、非对称错误约束的复合二元假设检验，给出了基于 Rényi 散度的显式可达界与反 converse 界，并刻画误差指数相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典假设检验结果多关注简单假设或渐近固定一类错误约束；复合假设、有限样本、以及一类错误随样本指数衰减时的最优二类错误行为仍需精确刻画。

Method: 利用 Rényi 散度与联合 Rényi 投影构造统一似然比检验；通过 KL 投影确定相变阈值，并对有限字母表上紧凸且满支撑的假设类分析误差指数、强反 converse 与多项式修正。

Result: 得到最优二类错误的显式可达与 converse 界；发现相变阈值由备择类与零假设类的联合 KL 投影给出；在相变两侧确定精确误差指数，并证明可达指数由唯一 Rényi 阶达到。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了复合 Chernoff--Stein 指数、有限样本可达性与强 converse 现象，并说明无需最不利分布对也可实现对两类复合假设的统一错误控制。

Abstract: We investigate composite binary hypothesis testing in the finite sample regime under asymmetric error constraints. Using Rényi divergences, we derive explicit achievability and converse bounds for the optimal Type II error. When the Type I error is constrained to decay exponentially with sample size, the bounds identify a phase transition and yield a strong converse above it. In the composite problem, the phase transition threshold is given by the joint KL projection over the alternative and null classes. Achievability is obtained through a joint Rényi projection whose log likelihood ratio defines a single test with uniform error control over both hypothesis classes, without requiring the projected pair to be least favourable. For compact convex classes with full support on a finite alphabet, we determine the exact error exponents on both sides of the transition and show that the achievable exponent is attained at a unique Rényi order. The same framework recovers the fixed Type I composite Chernoff--Stein exponent and yields a polynomial refinement of the finite sample achievability result. We further identify conditions under which the projected pair is least favourable at finite sample size.

</details>


### [215] [ODMA-based MIMO Massive Unsourced Random Access with Soft-Output Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28085)
*Tianya Li,Xiaoran Zhang,Nan Hu,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang,Xiang-Gen Xia,Chengshan Xiao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文面向MIMO大规模无协调随机接入系统，设计结合ODMA与软输出极化码的传输、检测与译码方案，以在短码长和高并发场景下提升可靠性并降低复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模URA系统中活跃用户未知、碰撞严重且短包传输对可靠性要求高；传统ODMA检测复杂度较高，且编码与检测之间的软信息利用不足，因此需要一种兼顾传输增益、编码增益、迭代增益和低复杂度的联合设计。

Method: 提出三段式导频解耦编码方案以降低数据段码率而不增加开销；构建分层模式检测框架，先通过相关运算获得粗粒度候选传输模式集合，再用基于消息传递的算法迭代估计模式后验概率，并用MAP准则精确判决；进一步利用极化译码器的比特级软输出信息，实现联合模式检测与数据译码，并基于SCL极化译码软信息设计MP迭代译码算法。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案具有较强鲁棒性，能够改善模式检测精度和数据译码性能，同时通过分层检测显著降低计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提方案有效融合了未耦合ODMA的传输增益、短码长极化码编码增益以及软信息迭代译码增益，适合MIMO大规模URA场景中的高可靠、低复杂度随机接入传输。

Abstract: This paper investigates the design of the on-off division multiple access (ODMA) transmission scheme for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) massive unsourced random access (URA) systems with soft-output (SO) polar codes. First, a three-segment pilot-uncoupled coding scheme is introduced under the ODMA framework, which reduces the coding rate of the data segment without increasing the transmission overhead, improving the overall system performance. Building upon this architecture, a hierarchical pattern detection framework is developed. Specifically, a coarse-grained candidate set of transmission patterns is first identified through correlation operations. Based on this, a message-passing (MP)-based pattern detection algorithm is developed to iteratively estimate the posterior probabilities of transmission patterns, followed by the \textit{maximum a posteriori} (MAP) estimation to obtain the precise pattern detection result. Furthermore, a joint pattern detection and data decoding algorithm based on the bit-wise SO information of polar decoder is investigated, where the posterior probability information provided by the polar decoder is exploited to refine the pattern detection and contribute to an improved accuracy. In addition, by leveraging bit-wise SO information of the successive cancellation list polar decoder, an MP-based iterative decoding algorithm is developed to significantly enhance the decoding performance. The proposed scheme simultaneously exploits the transmission gain of uncoupled-ODMA framework, the coding gain of polar codes in the short-blocklength regime, and the iterative decoding gain enabled by SO information, while the computational complexity is significantly reduced through the hierarchical detection framework. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves strong robustness ...

</details>


### [216] [Ada-TokenCom: Rate-Adaptive Token Communications via Large-Model-Driven Token Compression and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28086)
*Zijun Zhang,Li Qiao,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Zhen Gao,Mehdi Bennis,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ada-TokenCom：一种基于大型自回归模型的速率自适应 Token 通信框架，将下一 token 预测与算术编码结合，实现超低比特率的 token 级语义通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信需要在低比特率、时变信道条件下实现高效多模态语义/目标导向传输；TokenCom 以 token 统一通信与计算单元，但仍需解决自适应压缩与信道匹配问题。

Method: 采用预训练自回归大模型进行混合重建/生成：发送端编码并传输序列开头信息量高的 token，接收端用相同模型预测剩余 token；结合算术编码降低码率；设计基于 Lyapunov 优化的算法，动态联合调整源压缩率和调制编码方案。

Result: 仿真表明，Ada-TokenCom 在语义通信性能上优于传统数字通信基线和深度联合信源信道编码语义通信基线。

Conclusion: Ada-TokenCom 证明了基于大模型 token 预测、算术编码和跨层自适应控制的结合，可有效支持超低比特率、网络条件变化下的语义通信。

Abstract: Token Communications (TokenCom) has recently emerged as a new paradigm in which tokens serve as unified units for communication and computation, enabling efficient multimodal semantic and goal-oriented transmission. In this paper, we develop Ada-TokenCom, a rate-adaptive TokenCom framework based on large autoregressive models, which integrates next-token prediction with arithmetic coding to achieve ultra-low bitrate semantic communication at the token level. We propose a mixed reconstruction/generation scheme, where the transmitter encodes and transmits the highly informative tokens at the beginning of the token sequence leveraging a pre-trained autoregressive large model, while the receiver uses an identical model to predict the rest. Moreover, we design a Lyapunov-based algorithm to dynamically optimize both the source compression rate and the modulation and coding scheme, adapting to time-varying network conditions. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed Ada-TokenCom framework outperforms both digital and deep joint source-channel coding-based semantic communication baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Fine Difference Structure and Prime-Power Depth of Bent Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28133)
*Zhaorui Wu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明任意 p-元 bent partition 的深度 K 必为 p 的幂，并进一步给出全局上界 t≤⌊n/2⌋；核心是通过对平衡合并的精确有限平均得到零差平衡与差族性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 p-元 bent partition 中可实现的分区深度 K。此前关于 K 是否必须为 p 的幂的问题，只在正则性或单元对称性等附加假设下对一般 p 得到肯定结果；本文旨在去除这些限制，给出无条件结论。

Method: 作者对所有平衡 coarsening 进行精确有限平均计数，证明对任意非零平移 h，仍落在同一 fine cell 中的点数恒为 p^n/K。由此推出 fine cells 构成 partitioned difference family，且 fine label map 满足 zero-difference balanced。部分主要计数恒等式和推论使用 Lean 4 形式化并进行内核验证。

Result: 得到 K | p^n，因此 K=p^t；由于各 cell 非空，进一步有 1≤t<n。在偶数维情形，结合经典 cell-size 定理得到 K | p^{n/2}；再结合已知奇数维三元三纤维参数限制，推出全局界 t≤⌊n/2⌋。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 p-元 bent partition 深度是否为 p 的幂的开放问题，并建立更强的平移不变计数性质；其结果将 bent partition 与差族、零差平衡映射联系起来，同时通过 Lean 4 增强了关键计数论证的可靠性。

Abstract: A $p$-ary bent partition of $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ is a partition into $K$ nonempty cells such that every balanced assignment of its cells to $\mathbb{F}_p$ produces a bent function. It was asked whether every possible depth $K$ is a power of $p$; for general $p$, previous affirmative results required regularity or cell-symmetry hypotheses. We prove the stronger unconditional statement that, for every nonzero $h$, exactly $p^n/K$ points remain in the same fine cell under translation by $h$. Thus the fine cells form a partitioned difference family and the fine label map is zero-difference balanced. Consequently $K\mid p^n$, so $K=p^t$; nonempty cells further give $1\le t<n$. In even dimension, the classical cell-size theorem yields $K\mid p^{n/2}$. Together with the known odd-dimensional ternary three-fibre parameter restriction, this gives the global bound $t\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor$. The proof is an exact finite average over balanced coarsenings. The main counting identity and selected consequences are formalized and kernel-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [218] [Shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over Fq+uFq](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28222)
*Junmin An,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文给出了有限环 F_q+uF_q 上线性码最短自正交嵌入与 LCD 嵌入长度的精确刻画，并提供构造方法与示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性码通过添加坐标实现自正交或 LCD 性质时所需的最短嵌入长度，这对码的结构分类、构造及最优码搜索具有意义。

Method: 将 F_q+uF_q 上的 Gram 矩阵分解为有限域 F_q 上一对对称矩阵，并把嵌入问题转化为有限域上对称矩阵与交错矩阵的合同分类；利用 Witt 理论构造全部最短自正交嵌入，并用附加矩阵形式刻画最短 LCD 嵌入。

Result: 获得了最短自正交嵌入长度的完整公式，在偶特征和奇特征情形下均出现两个不同分类；证明任意具有非零自由秩的自正交码都可视为某个码的最短自正交嵌入；给出了最短 LCD 嵌入的完整矩阵刻画。

Conclusion: 论文系统解决了 F_q+uF_q 上线性码最短自正交与 LCD 嵌入问题，建立了长度公式、结构分类和构造方法，并通过示例展示了所得嵌入在最小距离和 Gray 像最优性方面的潜力。

Abstract: This paper determines the exact lengths of shortest self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings of linear codes over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$. By decomposing Gram matrices over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ into pairs of symmetric matrices over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$, the embedding problems are reduced to the congruence classification of symmetric and alternate matrices over finite fields. Complete formulas for the shortest self-orthogonal embedding length are obtained, with two distinct cases arising in both even and odd characteristic. We also show that every self-orthogonal code over $\mathbb{F}_q+u\mathbb{F}_q$ with nonzero free rank can be viewed as a shortest self-orthogonal embedding of another code. We use Witt theory to construct all shortest self-orthogonal embeddings. A complete characterization of shortest LCD embeddings is also established in terms of invertible and arbitrary matrices of prescribed sizes appended to a generator matrix. Examples of self-orthogonal and LCD embeddings with the largest minimum distance for the code considered are also presented, some of whose Gray images are optimal codes over $\mathbb{F}_q$.

</details>


### [219] [Significance-Driven Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28441)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Sirin Chakraborty,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以样本显著性为驱动的跨层语义通信框架，同时优化物理层语义编码/推理与MAC层资源分配，以提升语义频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通信优化通常关注比特级传输效率或整体任务性能，难以区分不同观测样本对下游任务的实际价值；在带宽、时隙和信道条件受限且动态变化的场景中，需要依据样本的任务相关语义价值进行跨层联合设计。

Method: 作者基于统计决策理论定义逐样本数据显著性的信息论度量，并以“单位带宽、单位时间传递的语义价值”定义语义频谱效率。物理层提出Meta-Learning Variational Information Bottleneck（Meta-VIB）语义收发机，通过元学习超网络将高维观测压缩为语义显著潜变量，并适应动态信道和符号预算。MAC层将信道分配建模为Multi-Action Restless Multi-Armed Bandit（MA-RMAB），采用Q-Maximization算法根据语义信息价值动态分配资源。

Result: 在真实行人安全数据集上的实验表明，该联合设计相较基线显著提升语义频谱效率；在平均SNR为0 dB时最高达到约1000倍增益，在平均SNR为5 dB时达到约40倍增益。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将样本级语义显著性引入物理层压缩推理和MAC层资源调度的联合优化，能够在动态无线环境中更高效地传递任务相关信息，并显著提高语义频谱效率。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a significance-driven cross- layer semantic communication design problem. Based on sta- tistical decision theory, we introduce an information-theoretic measure of per-sample data significance that quantifies the task-specific value of each individual observation. Using this metric, we formulate a cross-layer optimization problem that simultaneously optimizes (i) physical-layer semantic encoding and inference and (ii) MAC-layer resource allocation, with the objective of maximizing semantic spectrum efficiency, defined as the semantic value delivered per unit bandwidth per unit time. At the physical layer, we develop Meta-Learning Variational Information Bottleneck (Meta-VIB), a new semantic transceiver that employs a meta-learned hypernetwork to compress high- dimensional observations into semantically significant latents, enabling instantaneous adaptation to dynamic channel conditions and varying symbol budgets without online retraining. At the MAC layer, we model channel allocation as a Multi-Action Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (MA-RMAB) and adopt the Q- Maximization algorithm, which dynamically allocates channel resources to sensors based on their semantic value of information. Experimental results on a real-world pedestrian safety dataset demonstrate that our joint design achieves substantial gains in semantic spectrum efficiency over baselines, reaching up to 1000 times gain at an average SNR of 0 dB and 40 times gain at an average SNR of 5 dB.

</details>


### [220] [Distributed Cross-Layer Optimization for Covert Multi-Hop, Multi-Modal Networks: Exponentially Fast Convergence and Robust Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28469)
*Sirin Chakraborty,Andrea Panebianco,Yuchen Tian,Kevin S Chan,Fikadu Dagefu,Yin Sun,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向隐蔽多跳、多模态无线网络的分布式跨层优化算法，联合处理拥塞控制、路由、调度与功率控制，并通过凸化隐蔽性约束实现可证明收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐蔽无线网络中，监测者通过能量检测发现传输；检测错误概率（DEP）关于发射功率通常非凹，导致基于DEP的联合网络优化难以直接求解，尤其是在多跳、多模态和跨层资源分配场景下。

Method: 作者构造了log-DEP的最紧凹下界，将原始非凸隐蔽约束转化为保守凸问题，同时统一处理硬隐蔽性约束与隐蔽性-效用最大化；随后设计并分析并行近端ADMM（PP-ADMM）分布式算法求解该跨层问题。

Result: 理论上证明该算法在标准正则条件下全局Q线性收敛到最优解集；数值实验验证了线性收敛，并显示其在信道衰落和监测者移动条件下具有较好的跟踪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为隐蔽多跳无线网络中的跨层联合优化提供了首个分布式算法框架，通过DEP凹下界实现可 tractable 的保守凸优化，并兼具理论收敛保证与动态环境适应能力。

Abstract: This paper develops the first distributed cross-layer algorithm for joint congestion control, routing, scheduling, and power control in covert multi-hop, multi-modal wireless networks, where adversarial wardens (Willies) monitor radio modalities via energy detection. The Detection Error Probability (DEP), the probability that a Willie fails to reliably detect ongoing transmissions, is generally non-concave in the transmit powers, making DEP-based covert network optimization challenging. We resolve this by constructing the tightest concave lower bound on the log-DEP, yielding a conservative convex problem that guarantees satisfaction of the original DEP constraints and unifies hard covertness constraints and covertness-utility maximization in a single problem. We develop a Parallel Proximal Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (PP-ADMM) algorithm for the resulting cross-layer problem and prove global Q-linear convergence, i.e., exponentially fast convergence, to the set of optimal solutions under standard regularity conditions. Numerical results confirm linear convergence and demonstrate robust tracking performance under channel fading and Willie mobility.

</details>


### [221] [A Complete Characterization of Tensorizable $f$-divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28556)
*Rodrigo Cruz,Flavio P. Calmon,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究 f-散度在乘积分布下的张量化结构，证明所有可能的张量化公式都具有由单一参数刻画的多仿射形式，并识别出满足该张量化定义的全部 f-散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Csiszár 的 f-散度提供了统一且广泛的分布差异度量框架，但实际中常用的仅有少数几类，如 KL 散度、χ² 散度和平方 Hellinger 距离。它们的重要优势在于对乘积测度具有简单的组合公式，即张量化性质。论文旨在系统刻画哪些 f-散度具备这种性质，并澄清张量化公式的可能形式。

Method: 作者在已有文献的张量化形式化框架基础上进行细化，建立更精确的张量化定义；随后从函数方程或结构约束角度分析乘积测度下 f-散度组合公式的形式，证明任意张量化公式必须是由单一参数决定的多仿射形式，并据此分类所有可张量化的 f-散度。

Result: 论文证明了在所采用的张量化概念下，任何合法的张量化公式都具有统一的多仿射结构，并且该结构由一个参数完全刻画；进一步给出了所有满足该张量化性质的 f-散度的完整识别结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为 f-散度的张量化性质提供了统一的结构定理和分类结果，解释了为何 KL 散度、χ² 散度和平方 Hellinger 距离等少数散度在信息论和统计中具有特殊地位，并为选择或构造可组合的分布差异度量提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Csiszar's formulation of the $f$-divergence introduced a vast family of functionals for quantifying dissimilarity between probability distributions. However, many applications in statistics and information theory rely only on a few $f$-divergences, such as the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the $χ^2$-divergence, and the squared Hellinger distance. These divergences are especially useful because they admit simple compositional formulas under product measures, a property sometimes referred to as tensorization. In this work, we refine a formalism of tensorization previously introduced in the literature. Then, we show that any possible tensorization formula has a multi-affine form characterized by a single parameter, and identify all tensorizable $f$-divergences under our adopted notion of tensorization.

</details>
